As always, the Friday before the Grand Prix had hundreds of players taking part in the last-chance Grand Prix Trials. Those grueling single-elimination tournaments were their last chance to earn two byes for the weekend's main event, but they were our first chance to get an idea of what decks to expect at the Grand Prix. Check out the winning decklists here!

#### Johan Disenborg, Grand Prix Trial Winner
